\vskip.4cm\vbox{\leftskip-3.65cm\noindent\ita%
Infra hebdomadam, si quavis die legatur evangelium de cæco nato, antiphonæ se\-quen\-tes
adhiberi possunt:
}\vskip.4cm%
\vbox{\advance\hsize by 3.65cm\hskip-3.65cm\rlap{Laudes}\hskip2.3cm\rlap{B}\hskip1.35cm\vtop{\hsize=9cm\ita {\reg\lv\pdfextension startlink attr {/Border [0 0 0]} goto name{A[4Q1,L,Ba]}Rabbi\pdfextension endlink}\nobreak\  ut supra pro anno A, ad\/ {\reg Benedictus.}}}\vskip.2cm%
\vbox{\advance\hsize by 3.65cm\hskip-3.65cm\rlap{Vesp.}\hskip2.3cm\rlap{M}\hskip1.35cm\vtop{\hsize=9cm\ita {\reg\lv\pdfextension startlink attr {/Border [0 0 0]} goto name{A[4Q1,2V,Ma]}A sæculo\pdfextension endlink}\nobreak\  ut supra pro anno A, ad\/ {\reg Magnificat.}}}\vskip.2cm%

\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Erat quidam regulus, cuius filius infirmabatur Capharnaum. Hic cum audisset quia Iesus veniret in Galilæam, rogabat eum ut sanaret filium eius.\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}

\vskip.4cm\vbox{\leftskip-3.65cm\noindent\ita%
Quæ antiphonæ etiam aliis annis adhibendæ sunt, si legatur evangelium de Lazaro.\hfill\break
Ubi tamen non cantantur antiphonæ propriæ ad Horas minores:
}\vskip.4cm%
\vbox{\advance\hsize by 3.65cm\hskip-3.65cm\rlap{Laudes}\hskip2.3cm\rlap{B}\hskip1.35cm\vtop{\hsize=9cm\ita {\reg\lv\pdfextension startlink attr {/Border [0 0 0]} goto name{A[5Q1,T,a]}Lazarus amicus noster\pdfextension endlink}\nobreak\   ut supra ad Tertiam.}}\vskip.2cm%

\vskip.4cm
%Nemo te condemnavit
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Nemo te condemnavit, mulier? Nemo, Domine. Nec ego te condemnabo; iam amplius noli peccare.\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}

\vskip.4cm
\vskip.4cm\vbox{\leftskip-3.65cm\noindent\ita%
Infra hebdomadam, si quavis die legatur evangelium de Lazaro, antiphonæ se\-quen\-tes
adhiberi possunt:
}\vskip.4cm%
\vbox{\advance\hsize by 3.65cm\hskip-3.65cm\rlap{Laudes}\hskip2.3cm\rlap{B}\hskip1.35cm\vtop{\hsize=9cm\ita {\reg\lv\pdfextension startlink attr {/Border [0 0 0]} goto name{A[5Q1,T,a]}Lazarus amicus noster\pdfextension endlink}\nobreak\   ut supra pro anno A, ad Tertiam.}}\vskip.2cm%
\vbox{\advance\hsize by 3.65cm\hskip-3.65cm\rlap{Vesp.}\hskip2.3cm\rlap{M}\hskip1.35cm\vtop{\hsize=9cm\ita {\reg\lv\pdfextension startlink attr {/Border [0 0 0]} goto name{A[5Q1,S,a]}Domine, si hic\pdfextension endlink}\nobreak\  ut supra pro anno A, ad Sextam.}}\vskip.2cm%
\vskip.2cm\goodbreak\line{\hskip-3.6cm\med Feria II\hfill}\nobreak\vskip.4cm%
\pdfextension dest name {QUAD57} fit
\vbox{\advance\hsize by 3.65cm\hskip-3.65cm\rlap{Laudes}\hskip2.3cm\rlap{Bab}\hskip1.35cm\vtop{\hsize=9cm\ita {\reg\lv\pdfextension startlink attr {/Border [0 0 0]} goto name{A[5Q1,L,Bc]}Inclinavit se,\pdfextension endlink}\nobreak\  de dominica præcedente pro anno C, ad\/ {\reg Benedictus.}}}\vskip.2cm%
%Qui sequitur me
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Qui sequitur me, non ambulat in tenebris, sed habebit lumen vitæ, dicit Dominus.\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}

%Ego sum qui testimonium
\vbox to 0pt{\pdfextension literal{q 1 g}\vbox to 0pt{\hbox to 0pt{Ego sum qui testimonium perhibeo de meipso; et testimonium perhibet de me, qui misit me, Pater.\hss}\vss}\pdfextension literal{Q}\vss}
